# Affinity Photo feature inventory (parity reference for Pinxit)

> **Provenance note (2026-09-26):** verbatim copy of the Affinity by Canva 3.3 inventory (the Pixel Studio and the photo studios that replace Affinity Photo 2) compiled for the Pinxit parity plan, rows `AF-0001` to `AF-2762`, under its original title: Affinity Photo feature inventory (Affinity by Canva, Pixel Studio and photo studios). The ids are stable and each is placed in exactly one row of [`../pinxit-parity.md`](../pinxit-parity.md). Do not renumber; a future Affinity version appends new ids after AF-2762 (see [`../README.md`](../README.md)).

> **Access limits:** affinity.studio began answering HTTP 429 to every client after about 200 of the 532 targeted help pages, so the remaining current pages were covered from the Affinity Photo 2 help (479 pages read in full). Some rows therefore carry Photo 2 wording; Photo 2 option detail is assumed to hold in the current app unless the current help replaces it, and rows marked "(Photo 2 only)" are documented for Photo 2 but absent or replaced in 3.3.

- Product: Affinity by Canva (single free app that unifies Affinity Photo, Designer and Publisher; Personas are now called Studios; photo editing lives in the Pixel Studio plus the Develop, Liquify, Tone Mapping, Retouching, Color Grading, Compositing, Astrophotography Stack, Canva AI and Slice/Export Studios). Native format .af; V1 and V2 .afphoto/.afdesign/.afpub open.
- Current version: Affinity 3.3 ("September 2026 Update"), released 2026-09-15 (build 3.3.0.4850 per release notes). Prior: 3.0 released 2025-10-30 (free app launch), 3.1 March 2026, 3.2 April 2026.
- Inventory date: 2026-09-26. Baseline for dropped features: Affinity Photo 2.x (rows marked "(Photo 2 only)" are documented for Photo 2 but absent or replaced in the current app).
- Purpose: parity checklist for Pinxit (GPL raster editor, WPF/SkiaSharp, Windows).

## Sources

- Current help center (Affinity by Canva): https://www.affinity.studio/help/ (per page https://www.affinity.studio/help/<slug>/)
- Affinity Photo 2 help: https://affinity.help/photo2/en-US.lproj/index.html (per page https://affinity.help/photo2/en-US.lproj/pages/<Dir>/<file>.html)
- September 2026 update: https://www.affinity.studio/blog/affinity-update-september-2026
- Release notes: https://www.affinity.studio/help/release-notes/
- CG Channel, Affinity 3.3: https://www.cgchannel.com/2026/09/canva-releases-affinity-3-3/
- CG Channel, Affinity 3.2: https://www.cgchannel.com/2026/04/canva-releases-affinity-3-2/
- Canva newsroom launch: https://www.canva.com/newsroom/news/all-new-affinity/

## Method notes

- The current help center navigation (709 pages in 65 categories) was harvested from the page JSON; 532 photo-relevant pages were targeted. About 200 were downloaded before affinity.studio started returning HTTP 429 to every client on this IP (curl, WebFetch and Chrome alike). The remaining current pages were covered from the Photo 2 help (479 pages downloaded in full, including every per-filter and per-adjustment page) plus release articles. Rows whose Source is an affinity.studio slug that could not be downloaded were checked only against the Photo 2 page and release notes, so current wording may differ.
- Photo 2 option detail was assumed to still apply in the current app unless the current help explicitly replaces it. "(Photo 2 only)" is used sparingly and only where the current help shows a replacement.
- Vector-only (shape drawing, text layout, page layout, EPUB, data merge) features are out of scope except where they serve raster editing (vector masks, pen and node tools, text tool rows).
- Kinds: tool, tool-option, command, panel, panel-option, adjustment, filter, filter-option, persona (studio), dialog-option, format, preference, behavior. Categories: core, ai, cloud, automation, video, print, format. Destructive filters and live filters are listed as separate rows where both exist.
- Validation: every table row was checked by script for exactly 7 columns, no pipes inside fields and no em or en dashes.
